| `4ae043b9`, superseded by `e01bd0de` | **Do not consume `4ae043b9`'s mechanism claims; a12-dmkits falsified them at `e01bd0de`.** What survives is the verdict-only measurement: `AggMalformedDiffTest.theMalformedCellsPositionDoesNotChangeAnAllRowsAggregateVerdict` reports the four all-rows aggregates suppressed with the malformed cell at head and at tail, with an all-valid positive control and a row column pinning that the swap was applied. Withdrawn at `e01bd0de`: the ordered first-cell return as a witnessed mechanism (verdict identity is what an ordered stop and a whole-selection survey both predict, and `RuntimeController` does survey a whole repetition pre-loop under `sparsenessOptimization`), the "gate the operator consumes" predictive rule (all these operators share one gate; the discriminator is `Combiner.canChange()`), and "position cannot be read off validation output" (the formal error names the row, so only the verdict is position-blind). The `Having` side channel narrows too: `MVK_DISALLOWED_CONSTRUCT_IN_FILTER_CONDITION` only when the filter carries another term, else `MVK_NO_ITERATION_FOR_WILDCARD`. Reviewed under [`SPEC-2026-08-06-02`](A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md#spec-2026-08-06-02--the-aggregate-stop-is-unwitnessed-and-the-relevance-gate-does-not-predict-an-operators-third-state-behavior) |
| `677e2eb7` | **The compute-side fixed multi-group filled count inverts its own validation reading.** A seeded Number target computed directly from `NumberOfFilledGroups(Alpha, Beta)` with one formally invalid operand group takes the ordinary exact count `VALUE|2` — the unavailable group counts as **filled** — while two empty groups yield `0`, on both kernel strategies and in the interpreter. So `ERRORED`, `VALUE|0`, and `CLEARED` are all refuted for the count. The mechanism is presence-versus-read, discriminated in the same compute run: `convertInvalidFieldsForCalculation` leaves the invalid cell merely present, and a value-reading twin over that same cell yields no value while the count yields `2`. Validation's general compute-poison rule is unaffected because it governs reads. Uncalibrated and not to be cited as measured: the twin reported `CLEARED` rather than `ERRORED`, and the probe cannot separate a clearing read from an aborting one. Structural and call-local group errors and repeatable operands are uncovered. Answers accepted [`EXP-2026-08-06-01`](A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md#exp-2026-08-06-01--a-fixed-multi-group-filled-count-may-be-special-cased-ahead-of-computation-poison); locked by `FixedGroupCountComputeDiffTest`, recorded upstream as `KF177` |
| `6fe8d501`, `12491e4f` | **Per-operator partial-validation relevance, measured against the Kernel rather than carried by an operator list.** `Sum`, `MaxValue`, `MinValue`, and `NumberOfDifferentValues` are silent under concretely enumerated rows and fire under a wildcard; `SumOfProducts` and `FirstFilledValue` fire under both, because each owns its loop and reaches neither the shared combiner nor its pre-loop whole-repetition survey. The wildcard column is the positive control, and the reading is what the Kernel itself reports per operator rather than agreement between two engines. `SumOfProducts` third-state behavior is measured in the same batch: a formal failure in either zipped list suppresses, position does not change the verdict, and the formal pass emits every invalid cell regardless of the rule, so no ordering claim has a witness in validation output. This corrected a real divergence here, not only in the peer's interpreter: this project had inherited the wildcard-only gate for `SumOfProducts` from the aggregate family. Locked by `PartialValidationAggregateDiffTest.everyAllRowsAggregateNamedByTheRelevanceClaimIsMeasured` and `AggMalformedDiffTest` |
| `5f1c08dc`, `618d2847` | `FieldValuesNotUnique` admission gates read the **whole** operand list, so authored position never selects the reported diagnostic class — the kind and format gates each measured with the pre-empted code asserted absent and with the operands reordered (`FieldValuesNotUniqueAdmissionLawsTest`). And the temporal compared identity is the **stored text** (`feldWertIntern`, a source-level reading), with its agreement with the decoded date carried as an explicit assumption: at the widest legal format `yyyy` and across the cross-kind `Date`-beside-`DateFragment` pair the format gate admits, every near-miss spelling is refused at input checking, co-checked on static Java and dynamic Groovy. A measured negative, not a proof that no witness exists. Reviewed under accepted [`SPEC-2026-08-05-04`](A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md#spec-2026-08-05-04--a-field-list-admission-gate-applies-to-the-whole-operand-list-so-authored-position-does-not-select-the-diagnostic-class) and [`SPEC-2026-08-05-03`](A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md#spec-2026-08-05-03--the-temporal-fieldvaluesnotunique-identity-is-stored-text-and-its-agreement-with-decoded-values-is-an-assumption-rather-than-a-measured-equivalence) |

Accepted and pending locally originated changes remain in [`A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md`](A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER.md). A reviewed inbound correction is recorded here and in the canonical spec; it does not create a new outbound entry.

## Maintenance rule

Update this file only when an authoritative locus, reusable drill route, source tension, or inbound provenance checkpoint changes. Do not append Lean implementation status, current exclusions, capsule summaries, theorem/test counts, or a chronological research narrative. Replace superseded source guidance and rely on Git for the old review path.
